  4838: 
  4839:   List<Map<String, dynamic>> _normalizeSkuRows(
  4840:       List<Map<String, dynamic>> rows) {
  4841:     return rows.map((item) {
  4842:       final row = Map<String, dynamic>.from(item);
  4843:       if (_isBackendSettledSkuRow(row))
  4844:         return _normalizeBackendSettledSkuRow(row);

  4846:           row['marketplace_sku_id'] ?? row['remote_sku_id'] ?? row['sku_id'],
  4847:           '');
  4848:       final marketplaceSku = _text(
